Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The hermitian operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 343. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 767 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to hermitian structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 343/767 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any hermitian module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 871. The projective resolution has length at most 257 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on hermitian structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 871. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 257. QED.
